# v0.7.1 → 0.8.0 — Model completeness: Ledger

The working record of [the plan](todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-plan.md) ("the plan"). One writer: the orchestrating
session; lanes, verifiers, tie-breakers, implementers and reviewers report to it. `scripts/check_ledger.py` checks
the triage (every item has a destination), the register's ids and kinds and, from M4, `--final` (the plan's M4 gate).
The limits the maintainer last set (the plan's *Usage*): on 2026-11-09 four agents at once, six where two of them
run no `cargo`, and two build slots, the gate and one `-p` build.

## Release set (D1)

Researched 2026-10-29 (the research report's proofs go into `docs/facts/sources.toml`). Decided by the plan's rule,
the newest stable release set at M0, coherent:

- **KDE:** Plasma 6.8.2, KDE Frameworks 6.31.0, Qt 6.12.0, PySide6 6.12.0, in the Arch Linux Archive snapshot that
  carries them, 2026/10/29 (`archlinux@2026-10-29` in `sources.toml`), with kirigami-addons 1.15.0: 1.16.0 came out
  the same day and no snapshot carried it when the release set was pinned (the 2026/10/30 snapshot was still not
  published on 2026-10-30), so it is outside the frozen set.
- **GNOME:** libadwaita 1.10.0, GTK 4.24.1, gsettings-desktop-schemas 51.0; the same snapshot as KDE carries them.
- **Material:** material-web v2.5.0; Compose Material 3 1.5.0-beta02 (androidx `e4d933c9`, released 2026-10-21;
  re-pinned on 2026-10-30 from 1.4.0): R1.6 makes Compose the reference "experimental or not (for some Expressive
  behaviour it is Google's only implementation)", and the Expressive components the specification cites
  (FloatingToolbar and the others) exist only in the 1.5.0 pre-releases; a published pre-release is a release a
  harness can run, which is what D1's rule against `master` commits guards; material-color-utilities by commit (it has
  no tags); the emulator image: Android 17's stable API 37.1 exists only as a 16 KB page-size image, so spike A
  chose API 37.1 rev 9 (2026-10-30).
- **Windows:** Windows App SDK 2.5.1 with microsoft-ui-xaml `winui3/release/2.5.1`, the newest pair with a WinUI tag
  (2.6.4 is newer but microsoft-ui-xaml has no tag for it), so the WinUI host builds against 2.5.1 too; the Windows
  Community Toolkit 8.2.251219; Microsoft Learn pages at MicrosoftDocs/windows-dev-docs' commit.
- **Documentation pages** are snapshotted per page when a lane first cites one (a `snapshot` entry in `sources.toml`,
  which `check_sources.py` and `update_sources.sh` gain with the first): today's Apple pages describe 27, so for a 26
  fact only an archived snapshot from 26's lifetime, the pinned Xcode headers or a measurement serves (D12).
- **Apple (D12):** `macos-26` (macOS 26.6.2, Xcode 26.6, the iOS 26.5 simulator) and `macos-15` (macOS 15.7.9, Xcode
  16.4, the iOS 18.6 simulator); the images move within a release, so each evidence file records its exact build.

## Re-pin (M0)

`scripts/repin_citations.py`, applied 2026-10-30 (2218dc32): of 5,730 in-scope citations, 1,701 unchanged, 2,950
moved and 516 present at the pin were rewritten or kept as they are (3,039 citations and 44 source definitions
rewritten); 78 changed and 14 vanished go to the orchestrator's re-read (below); 471 unmappable and 46 held back
(their cell shares a ref with a changed or unmappable citation) are listed in
[`todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-repin-unmapped.tsv`](todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-repin-unmapped.tsv), each
re-read at its pin by the lane that takes its row. The changed and vanished were re-read (2026-10-30): 54 had moved
to other lines and 80 stale refs were reworded (applied); 38 state behaviour that changed or is gone at the pin,
listed in [`todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-repin-changed.tsv`](todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-repin-changed.tsv) for
the lanes (11 of them on rows traced `out`); one reaches the catalogue (queue #16).

inventory*): in [`todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-inventory.md`](todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-inventory.md)

part, their callers being the reader's).

| 4f88c46b + 7d703911's cherry-pick | M0 | 178 ✅ 3 ⚠️ 0 ❌ (181 checks, 11 min) | `cargo outdated` cannot resolve `zbus`'s `blocking-api` feature in its temporary workspace (as in 0.7.0's release runs); visual assets and compatibility claims stale, warnings until M6 dates the CHANGELOG entry |
| 5273d9dc (task 17e's tree; two wording fixes folded in after the run, HEAD e84479f0, the checks they reach passing again) | M1a | 105 ✅ 100 ⚠️ 0 ❌ (205 checks, 42 min) | the 97 `PLAN_EXCLUDED` steps (the expected list above, *`PLAN_EXCLUDED`*); `cargo outdated`, the visual assets' stamp and the compatibility claims' stamp, as at M0 |
| b254a561 (the run), 73a957f7 (its fix and the steps it did not reach) | M1b | at b254a561: 107 ✅, then ❌ at the 108th step, where the run stops (8 h 32 min at 8 gate threads, peak 7.65 GB; the facts gate over every preset in one step, 459 min 17 s): `docs, private items (native-theme, Linux features)`, a link in `validate::synthetic_shape`'s docs to `check`, a test-only function, which no CI job documents; fixed at 73a957f7, the step passing there. The steps after it, run at 73a957f7 one by one with the script's own commands for the crates `PLAN_EXCLUDED` does not name: docs.rs emulation (six crates), the core package, semver-checks (`native-theme`, `native-theme-build`), all ✅. Between the two commits the facts, the presets, the documents and their scripts changed, and of the code the generated widget code (`widgets/gen_button.rs`, `gen_index.rs`) and `query`'s mirror with its tests (ADD-A146's code follow-ups): covered at 72dd07b4 locally by the data checks (31 ✅), the facts gate on three of the five presets that changed (`kde-breeze`, `macos-monterey`, `macos-tahoe`; `ios-13` and `ios-26` gated by CI alone) and ten code checks (formatting, the panic scan, the gate's cfg check, clippy, the strict panic lints, the library's tests, docs, and the macOS and Windows cross-checks), and by CI's jobs and whole gate there (run 42755457912) | `cargo outdated`, the visual assets' stamp and the compatibility claims' stamp, as at M0; the `PLAN_EXCLUDED` steps not counted (the run stopped before its summary) |

| 42 | 3 | rationale R3 (fixed precedence), R25.3; spec S7.2/S10; catalogue `material-3` groups | Compose shows the more recent of hover and focus (material3-ripple `Ripple.kt:279-280`) and draws the press ripple with them, so the catalogue's exclusive group `[pressed, focus_visible, hover]` is wrong and no fixed precedence expresses recency | the Material lane | applied (ADD-A124); checked nine times, each check's findings fixed, the ninth with nothing serious: converged 2026-10-30 (444e664d); the checkers, the code (M1a, closed at e4d269be) and the lanes' facts (#81) followed; decided by the sources (CONTRA-6): a selector term on the most recent flag of a named set, not a fixed order and not an exclusive group. Compose keeps each set of interactions in the order they began and shows the last one: the ripple's state layer over hover, focus and drag, the press ripple drawn alongside (`Ripple.kt:250-280`); a button's elevation over hover, focus and press (`Button.kt:1703-1747`; also `Card.kt`, `FloatingActionButton.kt`, `Chip.kt`, `ToggleButton.kt`): an elevated button hovered after focus is at level 2, focused after hover at level 1 (`ElevatedButtonTokens.kt:43-74`). Any fixed order is wrong for one of those two sequences. An exclusive group cannot state them either: it drops flags for every property, and pressed is in the elevation's set but drawn alongside the state layer. Decision: the preset names each set, cited from the platform's code; a rule's term "`hover` latest in the set `elevation`" matches when the flag is present and was set after every other present flag of the set; the connector reports the order it set the element's flags in (element data, R25.3; it has it from setting them); flags with no reported order count by the set's stated order, its first as the latest, so the present order (focus over hover, as Material's View library ranks them) is the tie-break and a connector that reports nothing gets today's answer. Material's `[focus_visible, hover]` exclusive groups go: a layer rule matching only its latest flag already draws one layer. The gate (S17) checks each order of the flags present. Rejected: an element-wide most-recent group (fails on pressed, above); the connector dropping the older flags itself (preset data a generic connector does not have). Rationale R3 and R25.3, the spec and the catalogue follow in ADD-A124 |

| 49 | 3 | rationale R7 ("a radius per corner") | K1.26: Breeze draws a non-square RoundButton as an ellipse (Qt clamps each axis), which a radius per corner cannot state; elliptical corners (two radii per corner) would change R7's decision. Recommendation: keep R7 and record a non-square RoundButton as unexpressible (RoundButton is square in Kirigami's use) until a real need appears | the ADD-A121 draft | applied (ADD-A124); checked nine times, each check's findings fixed, the ninth with nothing serious: converged 2026-10-30 (444e664d); the checkers, the code (M1a, closed at e4d269be) and the lanes' facts (#81) followed; decided by the sources: R7 gains one corner kind, `Ellipse`. The catalogue's `circular` on `kde-breeze` is both Kirigami's RoundButton and a QWidget button carrying `_kde_round_button` (spec-widgets `button`, group 6). Kirigami's is a capsule: qqc2-desktop-style's own `Rectangle` with `radius: controlRoot.radius` (`RoundButton.qml:83`), which defaults to half the smaller side (`qtdeclarative@6.12.0 src/quicktemplates/qquickroundbutton.cpp:48`), `Full` exactly. Breeze's QWidget path gives `drawRoundedRect` half the larger side (`breezehelper.cpp:727-738`), which Qt clamps per axis (`qpainterpath.cpp:3457-3470`): the box's inscribed ellipse whenever the box is not square. KDE's own software sets the property on no non-square button in the pinned sources (Breeze's edit overlay is square, `breezestyle.cpp:5455-5466`, `:5510`), but an application using native-theme reaches the case by giving a `circular` button a label or a stretched box, and the model must state what is drawn ("cover everything visible"). Options: record it as unexpressible (a visible deviation on a reachable case); two radii per corner, CSS's `a / b` (a field more on every corner of every preset and connector, for one shape); `Ellipse`, each corner a quarter of the box's inscribed ellipse, half the width across and half the height down, equal to `Full` on a square box: chosen, the smallest exact form. All four corners or none, with `Circular` curves (S12 refuses a mix); its shadow and a `FollowPart` focus ring follow the ellipse of their own boxes. The trace's K1.26, #48's wording and the catalogue's group 6 follow in ADD-A124 |

ADD-A122's drafter recommends an optional `from` state set on `Transition` | the Material and KDE reconciliations, ADD-A122 item 19 | applied (ADD-A124); checked nine times, each check's findings fixed, the ninth with nothing serious: converged 2026-10-30 (444e664d); the checkers, the code (M1a, closed at e4d269be) and the lanes' facts (#81) followed; decided by the sources: an optional `from` flag set on `Transition` (ADD-A122's recommendation). Compose chooses the spec by the interaction entered, or, moving to rest, by the one left, and snaps from none or into disabled (`Elevation.kt:46-62`, `:80-104`; `Button.kt:1750-1770`). Breeze's pen fades only on leaving hover, visible focus or press, and its fill only on leaving press, into whatever state follows, checked included, while checked, default and window-activation changes are instant (`breezehelper.cpp:655-725`, `breezestyle.cpp:4545-4550`). Both are "the style after the change governs" with each of its transitions limited to the states left that it names: Breeze's base style states its fill transition `from {pressed}` and its stroke transition `from {hover, focus_visible, pressed}`, which a checked rule without transitions inherits; Material's rest elevation 120 ms `from {hover}` and 150 ms `from {pressed, dragged, focus_visible}`. A transition with no `from` applies from any state; `from` is matched against the element's flags before the change, after its exclusive groups' drop; among the matching transitions for a group the later in stated order wins, as now, which decides the one case Compose decides by recency (two flags lost in one update). Rejected: per-(from, to) pairs (R9's reason holds: the style entered already names `to`); exit transitions on the style left (they need a second precedence rule against the entered style's). Rationale R9 and the spec follow in ADD-A124 |

| 73 | 3 | rationale R9 (platform curves), CONTRA-2; spec S2.7 | Qt's `QEasingCurve` types and libadwaita's `ease_out_cubic` are curves the toolkit computes in code with no control points in the source; Qt's in-out, sine and expo curves are no cubic Bézier at all. ADD-A123 puts them in `Timing::Platform` (`PlatformCurve::Qt`, `::Adw`), while R9 and CONTRA-2 limit platform curves to velocity-dependent ones. Recommendation: widen R9 to "a curve the toolkit computes in code" (the model cannot otherwise state these curves exactly); the alternative states the four simple quad/cubic curves and `ease_out_cubic` as their exact cubic Béziers, leaving only the non-Bézier ones as platform curves | ADD-A123's checks | applied (ADD-A124); checked nine times, each check's findings fixed, the ninth with nothing serious: converged 2026-10-30 (444e664d); the checkers, the code (M1a, closed at e4d269be) and the lanes' facts (#81) followed; decided by the sources: widen R9's platform curves to "a curve the toolkit computes by a function of its own" (CONTRA-7's provisional reading becomes the rule; CONTRA-2's velocity curves stay members). Options: the widening; the alternative of exact Béziers for the five curves that are Béziers; a sampled table (rejected: approximate); a general expression language for curves (rejected: a second evaluator for a closed set of named functions). The alternative does not avoid the widening: Qt's `InOutQuad`, `InOutCubic`, `InOutSine`, `OutExpo` and libadwaita's sine and expo curves are no cubic Bézier, so they are platform curves under either; it adds a second form for five curves and a derivation-quote checker, and the facts would carry control points no source writes. A connector whose toolkit takes only Béziers can be given the exact Bézier of those five by a helper beside `timing::evaluate` when one needs it, with no model change. Rationale R9, CONTRA-2 and CONTRA-7 follow in ADD-A124 |

## Unmeasured register

Values not measured yet and values that cannot be measured (the plan's *How values are checked*): the Id (`U` and a
number, never reused; S17's pending facts name it), the Kind, the key and combination, the attempts and their
evidence, the options. A row is of one of two kinds. **`b`, a measurement to make:** a harness or probe pass on a
pinned runner, an analysis of committed evidence, or a source read. It is its lane's queue, needs no decision and is
closed by the lane's next round, the measured or cited fact replacing the pending one; a row whose Options begin "a
facts edit, no pass" waits on nothing but the facts. **`d`, a decision:** a value with no route (no pinned source
states it and no supported runner can measure it), or a choice between a measurement, a model form and a catalogue
correction. The orchestrator settles a choice of model form from the sources and the project's rules, writes the
outcome in the Decision cell, marked "open to the maintainer's veto", and lists it in the report of the milestone or
unit that settled it (D6's in its veto list; this rule itself its item 58); it stands unless the maintainer vetoes it (the plan's D6, outcome 14;
ADD-A147); only a pure preference, or a value only the maintainer can supply (another
setup, real hardware), stays the maintainer's, its Decision cell empty until the maintainer decides. Rejected: every
`d` row the maintainer's, one by one (the maintainer's standing rule: settle by logic first, ask only real
preferences). A row that waits
on a class 1 or 2 change (the queue's, the plan's *Spec changes*) is the orchestrator's to apply, whatever its kind
(U422, U425, U519, U520). A row leaves the
table, its id retired, when the value is stated, measured, or made not to exist by the catalogue or the
specification; a decided row stays until then. `check_ledger.py` fails a Kind that is neither on every run, and
under `--final` every `b` row of the enabled units (a measurement not made) and every `d` row of them with an empty
Decision. The lane round that opened each row, the plan's second column, comes with the task at M3a's start (queue
#212).

Retired ids (their rows removed once measured or split; never reused): U2 (the macOS umbrella row, split into U101–U103, de9d0c8e), U73 (Compose's incoming elevation curve, stated from animation-core, ab321618), U119 (ios-26's fill under `@loading`, measured, 54e40d48), U147, U148, U149, U152 (adwaita's forms ADD-A130 gave: the check context's `template`, the status-page spinner a `spinner` element and no icon context, `hover_time` absent, the button icon's `from_icon`; published in the GNOME lane's alignment to ADD-A125–ADD-A130); U74, U75 (`material-3`'s menu buttons' transitions, now stated from Compose's buttons' motion as queue #96 decided, MAT-N8, in the Material lane's alignment to ADD-A125–ADD-A130, 706e2235); U101 (AppKit declares no status colour: the status roles absent, ADD-A130), U105 (the Apple presets' `token.text.font_dpi` is S9.4's 72, ADD-A125) and U179 (the Selection set's border absent, run 41195846264's selection captures showing no stroke), removed in the macOS lane's alignment to ADD-A125–ADD-A130 (e28df3df). U326, U327 and U334 (`macos-tahoe`'s icon set and themes, its elevation ramp and size classes, and the menu button's glyph absent, settled from the SDK headers in the macOS lane's round 2, 4c140c81), U450, U453 and U476 (the iOS accent roles, the window roles UIKit names no colour for, and mnemonics and focus visibility, settled from the SDK headers in the iOS lane's round 2, f3daeb1a) and U500 and U514 (`windows-11`'s double-click distance and height and drag threshold, and `input.accent` with its six ramp shades, measured on the windows-11-arm pin in the Windows facts lane's round 2, 35948bf1), each with no fact pending under it, removed at the merge of the queued document, script and clean-room branches. U237 and U462 (`macos-monterey`'s and `ios-13`'s letter spacings, stated from the HIG capture `hig-typography@2025-08-15`, ADD-A136, ledger queue #176), no fact pending under them, removed at hig-176's merge. U163, U177, U235, U236 and U238 (`macos-*`'s highlight colour unchanged by AppleHighlightColor, 859c2d15; every text style's and the button label's font width, 701813d9; `macos-tahoe`'s and `macos-monterey`'s selection text under Increase Contrast, 0ed586ed, d391815b; `macos-monterey`'s bordered pop-up padding, 701813d9; the macOS facts lane's rounds 4 to 6), U157, U400, U507, U510 and U554 (`windows-11`'s colour interpolation, caret and per-language fallbacks, measured on the WinUI host, 464014ef; `kde-breeze`'s label icon size, measured on the KDE render host, ba30badd; `material-3`'s focus visibility, measured on the Android host, 1053a958) and U129, U154, U173, U175, U257 and U470 (the iOS configuration styles' default size, 910c571d; `ios-26`'s dynamic corners, f316b191; the pop-up's content width and slack, its title's elision and padding, 63281058; the caret's blink, bb76fbf9; the iOS facts lane's round 3), each with no fact pending under it, removed at the merges of facts-macos-m1b4, facts-hosts-m1b and facts-ios-m1b3. The 35 `ios-13` letter spacings facts-ios-m1b3 added pending under U462 (its tight, loose, emphasized and extra-large styles) are stated from `hig-typography@2025-08-15` after its merge, as hig-176 stated the base styles', so U462 stays retired. U144, U146 and U311 (the macOS text styles' weights, the drawn face's `wght` coordinate by S2.8's *Weight*, ADD-A136, and `macos-tahoe`'s leading variants' style, axes and features, measured in the macOS tie-break round 7, 780d88cb), no fact pending under them, removed by ADD-A141 (`docs/facts/macos/tokens.toml`'s comment naming U311 pending is stale, a facts follow-up). U698 (BreezeClassic's no-accent pair in the accent run, S17 *A port's own roles*, ADD-A142: run 41158393408 had measured it, its `classic-none` pair, equal to `classic-none-fresh`, with its kdeglobals not kept; committed from the run's artifact to the evidence repository's facts-kde-r7 994fa972 and its 172 facts stated with `accent = "#00000000"`, the KDE round 7), no fact pending under it. U699 (the KDE reader's font DPI on a Plasma X11 session, whether the session's XSETTINGS `Xft/DPI` can differ from the X resources' `Xft.dpi`): the question no longer arises, as the KDE reader reports no font DPI on any session, the toolkit's X11 scale factor already carrying the setting (S9.4's *What the toolkit's scale carries*, ADD-A146; the read, its helper `qxcb_xft_dpi` and ADD-A141's open item withdrawn, queue #198), and Plasma 6.8.2 installs no X11 session (`plasma-workspace@6.8.2 login-sessions/CMakeLists.txt:3-7`); no fact was pending under it. U761 (the `destructive` record's `fill`, `standalone` and `foreground` on `macos-*` and `ios-*`, opened by ADD-A147's fix round N2): no witness at 0.8.0's depth; a read with the later unit of depth that brings a destructive button. No committed source or measurement shows AppKit or UIKit drawing a destructive colour (the headers name a hue for a destructive action, `xcode@26.6 AppKit/NSTableViewRowAction.h:30`, `UIKit/UIActionSheet.h:41`), so the record stays absent as the catalogue allows (spec S3; ADD-A147, decision F1-4). No fact was pending under it.

| slice | macos (macos-monterey) | lane 1,174 button facts (405 stated, 769 pending) plus roles and tokens (f7b1aab4), verifier 1,000 | 292 of the monterey combinations agreed before the tie-break; of the disagreements 861 lane-pending, 120 one-sided, 22 valued | 22 valued: 4 lane right, 4 verifier right, 14 equivalent; all 861 pending checked by class: 218 made measured (the probe's own bezel fit, `menu` at rest, toolbar geometry), 629 stand; 23 text-style weights and `role.@highlight` adopted; the capsule rule now needs a passing fit and a corner mean within 1 px, which corrected tahoe's `circular`, `pill` and shadowless corners (U143); padding re-measured lane-wide from the alignment rect (tie-break 5d48e80f) | 21 | 0 |

## Spikes and drift runs

| Spike or run | Result | Run URL | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| M — macOS probe's button pass | **yes** (2026-10-29): every state, size (extraLarge on 26) and appearance in a key window on macOS 15.7.9 (Xcode 16.4) and 26.6.2 (Xcode 26.6); `highlight:YES` equals a real press pixel for pixel; AppKit draws no hover look for push buttons (record hover as not drawn); runner screen 1× only, no GPU reported; not yet: the accent and contrast passes, focus, mixed, other bezels | [40969040283](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40969040283) | `tools/os-probe/src/macos/buttons.rs`, `--button-pass`, merged into `v0.8.0-tools` |
| G — GNOME harness and libadwaita vector generator | **yes** (2026-10-30): the container from the 2026/10/29 snapshot (libadwaita 1.10.0, GTK 4.24.1, schemas 51.0) as `ghcr.io/tiborgats/native-theme-gnome:2026-10-29`, pulled without credentials; the host (`tools/gnome-host`) under mutter's headless mode (GTK's Wayland backend; Xvfb rejected: X11 font settings, no shadow) with `GSK_RENDERER=gl`, PNGs byte-identical across runs and CPUs, every button state drawn, a `.node` file of the unrounded drawing beside each PNG; `tools/gnome-vectors` writes `adw_standalone`'s vector table (495 rows). For the lanes: only `adw_standalone` is public (route 1); `gtk_contrast_pick`, Adwaita's `name_index`, `shadow_extent`, `gtk_wheel_step` need route 2 or 3; take measured colours from the `.node` files (PNG rounding); font hinting keys read unset with portals off | [40972799598](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40972799598) | merged into `v0.8.0-tools` |
| K — KDE container and accent script | **yes** (2026-10-30): the container from the 2026/10/29 snapshot, its versions checked against the release set (Plasma 6.8.2, KF 6.31.0, kirigami-addons 1.15.0, Qt and PySide6 6.12.0), `ghcr.io/tiborgats/native-theme-kde:2026-10-29`, pulled without credentials (each build re-pushes the tag: evidence records the digest); `scripts/measure_kde_accent.sh` per D2, 11 pairs (BreezeLight, BreezeDark, BreezeClassic × no accent, two accents), kdeglobals identical across runs and CPUs; `tools/kde-host` renders Breeze via QML and Qt Widgets in every state, byte-identical. For the lanes: BreezeLight leaves no `ColorScheme` key (the default); `--accent-color` never stores `AccentColor`, so each accent fact records its accent; states measured with `AnimationDurationFactor=0`. For the accent run (S17): BreezeClassic has the accent title bar and no Header group, and lacks a colour group; a tinting scheme needs a pin (OxygenDark, oxygen 6.8.2); a title-bar scheme with a Header group ships in no KDE project (third-party candidates to pin by commit: SUSE-Cafe's `SUSECafe.colors`, which also tints; Win98Dark; SkyDark), chosen at M1b | [40975872811](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40975872811) | merged into `v0.8.0-tools` |
| I — iOS simulator host | **yes** (2026-10-30) for iOS 26 (macos-26: macOS 26.6.2, Xcode 26.6, SDK 26.5, simulator iOS 26.5 on iPhone 17 Pro) and iOS 18 (macos-15, now macOS 15.8.2: Xcode 16.4, SDK 18.5, simulator iOS 18.6 on iPhone 16 Pro): `tools/ios-host` renders UIButton's configuration styles (12 on 26 with the four glass styles, 8 on 18) in each state, light and dark, at each size; measured from `simctl io screenshot` (Liquid Glass renders; `drawHierarchy` adds specks on clear glass); iOS 18 byte-identical across runs, iOS 26 within 1 level at glass edges. For the lanes: `isHighlighted` draws nothing on `prominentGlass` and dims only the title on `glass` and `clearGlass`, so the glass press needs a real touch (XCUITest press-and-hold), which the iOS lane's harness adds; the job picks the newest runtime of the SDK's major version (macos-15 also carries 26.x runtimes); `mini` draws as `small` on iPhone; clear glass needs content behind it; screenshots can stall up to a minute after boot | [40984711960](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40984711960) | merged into `v0.8.0-tools` |
| W — microsoft-ui-xaml coverage and WinUI host | **yes** (2026-10-30): of 1,018 keys (the button in full, the colour roles, the slice's tokens, resources of ten sample widgets), the XAML at `winui3/release/2.5.1` states 326 and chains 571 to a literal (51 to the OS accent); 121 not stated (focus ring widths, RepeatButton timing and tooltip delays in C++; animation timings, the accent ramp and high-contrast colours from the OS; line heights and placement as rendered results). `tools/winui-host` (Windows App SDK 2.5.1, self-contained, its `Microsoft.UI.Xaml.dll` from the pinned commit) runs on `windows-11-arm` and records a MenuFlyout's and a ToolTip's frames reproducibly. For the lanes: cite `controls/dev/**/<Control>_themeresources.xaml`, not the opt-in `_perf2026` copies or the framework's pre-Fluent `generic.xaml`; the runner measures RepeatButton 500/33 against gap-map row W2-19's 250/250 (a gap-map correction); the runner's screen starts behind a Windows setup screen the host closes | [40979612808](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40979612808) | merged into `v0.8.0-tools`; the coverage report is [`todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-spike-w-coverage.md`](todo_v0.7.1_model-completeness-spike-w-coverage.md), lane input |
| A — Android host | **yes** (2026-10-30): the emulator image is API 37.1 rev 9 (Android 17's stable release, 16 KB pages; `android-system-image@37.1-r9` in `sources.toml`), booting under KVM on `ubuntu-24.04` in 25–35 s; `tools/android-host` (Gradle 9.8.2, AGP 9.5.0, Kotlin 2.4.21, Compose Material 3 1.5.0-beta02 with the Compose BOM's 1.13.0-beta02) renders five button styles × five states × light and dark with `captureToImage()`, byte-identical across runs and CPUs. For the lanes: the pressed state is Android's own ripple, measured, not computed from the 0.10 token; one activity per capture (a reused ripple drawer draws nothing); PNGs carry a 16 dp margin, the index gives the bounds; 1 dp = 2 px on the 320-dpi screen; the Compose foundation and UI the app runs (1.13.0-beta02) are one step past the source cache's tree (1.13.0-beta01): cite them at the runtime's release | [40983911425](https://github.com/tiborgats/native-theme/actions/runs/40983911425) | merged into `v0.8.0-tools` |

`scripts/check_ledger.py --final` (M4) takes S19.2's staged inheritance file as `--staged-file
docs/facts/staged/inheritance-bases.toml` (written by `scripts/convert_inheritance_rules.py` at M1a), and reads the
drift comparison from `docs/facts/drift/<release>.tsv`, which the drift run's `check_fact_citations.py --drift`
writes, one line per measured fact of the enabled units (the plan's M4, *The drift run*); this section records each
drift run's URL and counts, and holds no per-fact table. Until M4's tooling task builds the file and its reader
(queue #213), `--final` reports the drift rule as a finding.

## Tokens

Per milestone, in two measures (the D6 package's section 1, generated 2026-11-10 at b254a561). **Reported:** the sum
of the agents' reported usage, which is each agent's final context size and not what its responses used; D7's cap
(+15,000k for M0–M1b) and M0's row are in this measure. **Used:** the transcripts' sum over every response, input,
cache writes and output without cache reads, the one measure that sums what was used. From M1b on the D6 package's
tables are the record (*Tokens (the project's transcripts)*, per milestone, model and day); this table gains one row
at each milestone's close.

| Milestone | Tokens | Cap | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| M0 and the six spikes | about 7,230k (the agents' reported usage: research and inventory 528k, scripts and their fixes about 2,350k, the spec amendments' sixteen checks about 2,110k, the re-pin and its re-read 765k, the spikes 1,320k, hook tests 84k) | 15,000k for M0–M1b (D7); lifted by the maintainer on 2026-10-30 ("don't limit usage tokens, just do the job") | the spec checks cost about three times what one plan review did: each round found narrower format gaps until the closed lists moved into the checkers; the D6 package counts M0 at 8,107k reported (42 notifications) and 12,067k used |
| M1a slice core | reported 122,823k (465 notifications); used 296,990k (10,766,895k with cache reads) | none since 2026-10-30 | to its close at e4d269be (2026-11-06 18:43); 21,025 facts written by then; M1b's facts lanes began that morning, which moves about half a day of their work into this row |
| M1b slice complete (closed 2026-11-10) | reported 61,423k (180 notifications); used 132,431k (6,539,426k with cache reads) | none | 53,004 facts added (74,029 in all, 45,766 of them pending); M0 to M1b reported 192,353k against D7's estimate of 15,000k, about 12.8 times it, which is the one calibration the project has for its own estimates |

## Triage

Every open item of `docs/todo.md`, the catalogue's *Open items*, ROADMAP's *Later — the connectors* and *Also in the
0.7.x releases*, and the plans `docs/todo_gpui-full-theme.md` and `docs/todo_iced-full-theme-geometry.md`, with its
destination: a named milestone, unit or lane; `settled by the model`; `after the plan's releases` (formerly `after
0.8.0`: past 0.8.0 and every follow-up release, D13) with its reason; or `not this plan's work`.

Drafted by an agent on 2026-10-29 from the rules below and ruled by the orchestrator (the draft's 68 doubtful rows):
a showcase item that touches theme data, Widget Info, the element lists, the parity gates or the screenshots goes to
M5, other showcase behaviour after the plan's releases; a connector convenience helper for a value the toolkit takes
only through a builder is after the plan's releases, the contract table listing the value (S21.3); an item with parts for several destinations
keeps one row naming its main destination and, in its reason, the others; a reader item goes to M1b where the
slice's `ReaderState` needs it, else to the readers unit; items that appeared done were verified and closed in M0 (three, 2026-10-29: the CI legs, the egui widgets crate, the Widget Info audit; their rows removed). Closed at M1b
with their rows removed: *KDE: the live reader's disabled text colour* (2026-11-09, ADD-A145: fixed by `kde_effects`,
the KDE disabled-role facts and the ported reader; its switch outlines moved to *KDE: the switch's hover and disabled
thumb outlines*). At M1b's close (2026-11-09) every row whose destination was a finished milestone was checked
against the code and the facts. Closed, their rows removed (each item's closing line in
`docs/archive/todo_closed.md` names its commits): *material-3: the display names "Material 3" and "Material 3
Expressive"* (`PresetId::display_name`, a55cdcd2); *macOS: the reader and the SF Symbols loader on macOS 10.15 and
earlier* (f551c4ee, a55cdcd2); *Linux font DPI: the `xrandr` fallback has no facts row* (dropped, ADD-A140);
*Font DPI*'s "Unverified: do the Linux readers'" (ADD-A140, ADD-A135); the catalogue's open item on iOS 26's
further bar-item roles (`UIBarButtonItem.h` at xcode@26.6 adds none; the `button` entry says what it adds); and
ROADMAP's *Also in the 0.7.x releases*, which D11 had M0 rewrite and M0 left: rewritten as *The `[v0.7.x]` items*,
so it is no item any more. Given a later destination, with the reason in the row: the macOS watcher's
notification center (M3 unit readers), the gpui contract's hover and pressed label rows and macOS's unemphasised
selection text (M2.1), the window background macOS 14 and 15 draw (lane macos), libadwaita's high-contrast rules
(lane gnome), the Linux button order and `detect_platform` (M2.5), and the hook's examples exemption (M5). No row
names M0, a spike, M1a or M1b now.

**Identity** (what `scripts/check_ledger.py` matches): Source; Heading as written, hashes included; Item, the
opening words between `“` and `”` as a prefix of the item's words (`docs/todo.md`: an open `- [ ]` item under its
nearest `###` heading since the last `##`, the tag stripped; the catalogue: a `- ` bullet of *Open items* by its
first backticked name; ROADMAP: each `###` subsection and its `- ` bullets under *Later — the connectors*, and *Also
in the 0.7.x releases* as one item; the two connector plans: a fixed list the script holds). ` · l. N` is informational.
